# Page Parity Matrix

This matrix is the execution source for page restoration. A route existing is not completion.

| Legacy PHP | Legacy JS | Modern Route | Status | Legacy Tables Mentioned | Request Fields Mentioned | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Front/templates/admin/AlarmBar.php |  | global app header | partial - legacy alarm/message badge families, notification gates, manageSystem guard, static shell contract, and logged-in browser smoke restored; live push/Metronic audit remains | t_alarms, t_alarms_msg, t_alarms_medicine, t_alarms_birthday, t_alarms_assessment, t_alarms_medical, custom_notifications, custom_notifications_msg, custom_notifications_medicine, custom_notifications_birthday, custom_notifications_assessment, custom_notifications_medical, t_notification_setting, actions_control, actions_control_man, system_actions, system_actions_man |  | Restored the legacy top-right AlarmBar semantics in the modern desktop header: separate unread current-user badge families for Messages, Medication, Birthdays, Assessment, Medical report reminders, and General alarms; each family uses migrated unread receipt/message state, shows the latest four rows, keeps the legacy empty labels (`No New Messages` / `No New Alarms`), formats legacy-style dates, and links to `/alarms/msg`, `/alarms/medicine`, `/alarms/birthdays`, `/alarms/assessments`, `/alarms/medical`, and `/alarms`. The header now reads migrated `t_notification_setting` rows in legacy PHP index order for `$_SESSION['notif']` and hides Medication, Birthdays, Assessment, and Medical report families when their old `alarms` toggle is off; Messages and General remain always visible like `AlarmBar.php`. The legacy `manageSystem` ACTION grant now controls the user-dropdown `Admin Panel` link, `/users/admin` bridge, restored legacy admin settings pages, admin AJAX validators, and direct legacy-admin server mutations when the imported action catalogue is configured and not granted. Smaller layouts retain the combined inbox tray to avoid header overflow. The app layout now server-hydrates the initial header data so the legacy badge family tree is stable on first paint, avoiding the dev-browser hook mismatch that appeared when the client fetched header data after hydration. `src/scripts/verify-legacy-app-shell-contract.ts` now verifies the archived `AlarmBar.php` families, gate indexes, empty labels, see-all labels, source delivery tables, latest-four cap, modern header render hook, server-provided initial data, and Admin Panel guard. Logged-in browser smoke on `/dashboard` confirmed the six legacy badge buttons render and the Messages popover opens with `No New Messages` plus `See All Messages` without a visible error overlay. Remaining work is exact Metronic dropdown styling/hover behavior and websocket/live badge refresh parity. |

Copy/PDF/Excel/CSV export and print controls now use the legacy Quadaa column order while excluding the Action controls. |
| Front/templates/admin/Branch_Dashboard.php | Front/templates/admin/js/Branch_Dashboard.js | /Branch_Dashboard.php, /branches/[id]/dashboard | restored - legacy branch dashboard bridge, KPI filters, payments modal, drilldown modals, and drilldown export/print restored | auto, themeforest | from, id, to, year | Restored branch-scoped dashboard KPI groups for Total Classes, Total Active Children, staff counts, attendance, absence, missing daily/absence reports, payments, accidents, calls, medical reports, assessment totals/missing/drafts, plus branch-scoped children-per-class, gender, and attendance charts. `/Branch_Dashboard.php?id=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy branch ids into the modern branch dashboard. The branch dashboard restores legacy date range and scholastic-year switching through `/branches/[id]/dashboard?from=&to=&year=`, applies that range/year to KPI queries, preserves filter context in navigation links, and restores branch-scoped lazy modal drilldowns for legacy `getModal(type)` cards plus the `showmod()` Total Payments table with invoice print and receipt attachment actions. Legacy `Branch_Dashboard.js` WebSocket refresh was commented out, so no active `new_change` socket is required for page parity. Browser smoke confirmed the authenticated branch dashboard renders KPI cards, date/year controls, branch-scoped charts, operations cards, and the Total Payments drilldown modal with Export and Print controls and no fresh browser errors. Verified by `src/scripts/verify-legacy-branch-dashboard-contract.ts`. |
| Front/templates/admin/Child_Details.php | Front/templates/admin/js/Child_Details.js | /Child_Details.php, /children/[id] | partial - legacy dossier landing, child deep-link bridge, nested edit provenance, address coordinates, and edit wizard visual smoke restored; map picker and write-flow smokes remain | auto, themeforest | id | Restored `/children/[id]` as a real child dossier instead of redirecting to dashboard, with legacy progress flag wording, photo/name/status summary, child info, general care, address, parents, brothers/sisters, authorized pickup, previous garderie history, accounting setup, attachments, current location, and direct edit/print/dashboard/workflow actions. `/Child_Details.php?id=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, legacy key, and encrypted legacy child ids and renders the modern dossier page directly for legacy URL stability. `src/scripts/verify-legacy-child-edit-preservation-contract.ts` locks the legacy edit workflow and data-fidelity contract: the modern child edit form carries hidden record ids for existing addresses, siblings, relatives, and accounting rows, `updateChild` preserves existing nested child rows in place instead of deleting/recreating them, and the server-rendered dossier avoids client-only helper imports that break direct/legacy detail routes. Remaining work is full interactive Google map picker visual parity and final create/update/draft browser smoke. |

| Front/templates/admin/Doctor_Details.php | Front/templates/admin/js/Doctor_Details.js | /Doctor_Details.php, /employees/doctors/[id] | restored - legacy doctor detail/create form, field parity, visual smoke, bridge, and save workflow contract restored | auto, themeforest | id | `/Doctor_Details.php?id=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, legacy key, and encrypted legacy doctor ids and renders the modern doctor detail page directly for legacy URL stability, while no-id `/Doctor_Details.php` renders the modern doctor create page in place. The detail page now exposes a Legacy Profile snapshot with preserved legacy account provenance, Arabic name fields, register/license fields, contact/address, education/CNSS, placement, status, remarks, and language rows when present. The shared doctor create/edit form now restores the legacy `m_name`/Middle Name plus `f_name_ar`, `m_name_ar`, and `l_name_ar` Arabic name fields and persists them through validation, form hydration, and org-safe create/update actions. The legacy `uni_degree_ar` Arabic studied domain now persists as `Doctor.specializationAr` through the Prisma schema/migration, garderie doctor import, shared form, create/update actions, mapper hydration, doctor details, and Legacy Profile snapshot. Staff address edits now preserve existing staff address rows in place instead of deleting/recreating them, and keep the legacy Mouhafaza/Quadaa/Region/City/Street/Building field coverage through the shared form/action path. The action audit confirmed there is no active legacy ACTION gate for doctor detail/create/edit/delete behavior: the legacy doctor roster comments out the `addTeacher`/`updateTeacher`/`deleteTeacher` guards, active AJAX endpoints are `addDoctor`/`updateDoctor`/`deleteDoctor`, and imported SQL action catalogues do not define doctor-specific ACTION rows, so modern doctor writes remain org-safe and PAGE-guarded without adding a non-legacy teacher ACTION denial. The profile-photo/draft audit confirmed the legacy staff draft button is hidden, profile photo uploads and clears through `imageUrl`, and misleading legacy `delete*image` endpoints soft-disable attachment rows rather than the main profile photo. Runtime staff document saves now persist only rows with real uploaded or imported file URLs, preventing placeholder file paths in the legacy-compatible attachment tables. Verified by `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-middle-name-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-doctor-arabic-name-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-detail-bridge-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-address-preservation-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-action-audit-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-photo-draft-contract.ts`, and `src/scripts/verify-legacy-doctor-specialization-ar-contract.ts`. Verified by `src/scripts/verify-legacy-staff-detail-visual-smoke-contract.ts`. |

| Front/templates/admin/classesperbranch.php | Front/templates/admin/js/classesperbranch.js | /classesperbranch.php, /branches/[id]/classes | restored - branch-scoped class listing, filters, TableTools export, print, lightbox preview, ACL, bridge, and locked branch table restored | t_branch, t_class, auto, themeforest, system_actions, actions_control | brid, ids, name, lname, language, dob, maxStudents, from, to, order_date_from, order_date_to, q | Restored the legacy branch-scoped Classes Listing: `/classesperbranch.php?brid=` resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy branch ids; missing `brid` falls back to `/classes`; supported legacy filter aliases are preserved into the modern branch route; the page shows `Classes Management For Branch: {branch}`, `{branch} Branch Classes Listing`, New Class, checkbox select-all, S.N., Image, Class, Language, Max Students, locked Branch column, Date, Action, per-column filters, created-date range, Clear, 10/20/50/100/150/All page sizes, Copy/PDF/Excel/CSV export, print, legacy image fallback, clickable image preview/lightbox, update dialog, class dashboard action, and legacy-style soft deactivate. Class `addClass`, `updateClass`, and `deleteClass` ACTION grants now hide New/Edit/Delete controls and deny the same branch-scoped class write mutations when the imported action catalogue is configured and not granted. Browser smoke confirmed `/classesperbranch.php?brid=` redirects to `/branches/[id]/classes`, loads real branch-scoped rows with legacy headers and locked branch context, keeps the restored `ClassPhoto/default.jpg` fallback unbroken, opens the class image preview dialog, and exposes Copy/Excel/CSV/PDF export plus print. Verified by `src/scripts/verify-legacy-branch-classes-tabletools-contract.ts`. |
| Front/templates/admin/dailyreport.php | Front/templates/admin/js/dailyreport.js | /dailyreport.php, /daily-reports/[id]/edit, /daily-reports/new?childId= | partial - legacy daily report edit/create bridge, health/items flags, direct approval, progress, and supplemental fields restored; layout audit remains | auto, themeforest | date, fid, id, isdraft | The main and draft listings now link into the modern edit route with legacy child/report context preserved through migrated IDs and report dates. `/dailyreport.php?fid=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy daily-report ids into the modern edit form; `/dailyreport.php?id=` resolves raw numeric, UUID, legacy key, and encrypted child ids into a preselected new daily report and preserves `date=` as the modern report date. Daily report create/edit child selectors now load uncapped active children for production-sized rosters. The create/edit form now restores the legacy Constipation health flag and Daily Needs item flags for Wipes, Brush, Towel, Diapers, Baby Bottle, and Milk; imported rows hydrate from `constipation`, `wipeschecked`, `brushchecked`, `towelchecked`, `diaperschecked`, `babybottlechecked`, and `milkchecked`, and new saves write the same legacy keys into `DailyReport.legacyData` for parent native `newdaily.php` compatibility. The form also restores legacy Early Dinner (`lunch_id2`, `lntime2`, `lunchf2`), second/third nap windows (`sleep_from1`, `sleep_to1`, `sleep_from2`, `sleep_to2`), and noon mood (`mood2`) while preserving the modern first-mood field; migrated food rows write old numeric `fid` values into `lunch_id2` when available. Direct Submit/Save now follows the legacy `t_settings.direct_approval` gate, admins/managers can still submit directly, submitted reports no longer expose Save As Draft, update saves carry the requested draft/submitted status, and runtime saves preserve legacy `status`, `is_rep_draft`, and PHP-compatible `d_progress_all` completeness values. Daily report attachment import fidelity now preserves active `t_daily_attachments` title, URL, source database/key/id/table, and legacy daily report id, with orphan/invalid/no-file/existing rows counted in skipped totals. The `dailyreport.js` attachment workflow audit now locks legacy default `images/RepDocs/default.jpg` previews, `Image Title` capture, existing `att_title`/`rattid` hydration, required file+title validation for new attachments, existing-attachment preservation/removal, and modern `daily-report` scoped uploads/removal ids where legacy `att_title` is stored as the display filename. Verified by `src/scripts/verify-legacy-report-attachment-import-contract.ts`, `src/scripts/verify-legacy-daily-report-field-contract.ts`. Remaining work is final layout polish. |

Message list/thread/send payloads now share parser-safe legacy header, list item, numeric-key thread, empty-thread, and failed-send fallback shapes, including database-connection fallbacks for native PHP aliases. Parent alarm feeds now share a reusable contract assertion for empty header arrays, string id/status/datetime/href/date/event fields, newline-cleaned details, birthday template rendering, medical status/href omission, event `active ` compatibility, and native POST database-connection fallbacks. Push-token registration now shares a reusable contract assertion for exact legacy result strings, `show` array rows with string-safe token fields, legacy OS mapping, soft-delete strings, and database-connection fallbacks. `notifications_master` payloads now share a reusable contract assertion for the iOS-forced top-level object, `info`, `notification1` through `notification11`, string group names, array details, string detail fields, PHP quote cleaning, and database-connection empty eleven-group fallback. `src/scripts/verify-parent-native-parser-fields.ts` now codifies the iOS/Android required field keys and scalar/array types across login, daily/newdaily, absence, finance, food, holiday, notifications, garderie bootstrap, and message compatibility helpers. `src/scripts/verify-parent-credentialed-native-e2e.ts` now selects a high-coverage active parent and performs credentialed direct legacy route-handler E2E using bearer + `usites` for `/ws/login.php`, `/ws/daily.php`, `/ws/newdaily.php`, `/ws/absence.php`, `/ws/finance.php`, `/ws/foodcalendar.php`, `/ws/holcalendar.php`, `/ws/holcalendarOLD.php`, `/ws/messagesList.php`, `/ws/sendMessage.php`, `/ws/message.php`, `/ws/pnotifications.php`, birthday/medicine/insurance/vaccination/payment/missing-report/assessment/event/general `/ws/*_alarms.php` feeds, and `/ws/notifications_master.php`, asserting real row counts and parser-safe item fields where local migrated rows exist, creating/replying/opening/cleaning up a temporary parent thread, registering/showing/deleting/cleaning up a temporary iOS push token, validating parser-safe empty alarm headers when the local migrated DB has no alarm rows, validating unauthenticated no-pid `general_alarms.php` exposure, preserving PHP-compatible encrypted `Medical_form1/2/4.php?id=` missing-report hrefs for staff/admin links while keeping parent native medical rows href-free, plus all eleven iOS notification groups. Mobile-flagged staff-to-parent message sends now use active tokens for provider-neutral OneSignal/webhook delivery attempts. Generated event alarms now write idempotent parent/mobile `custom_notifications_events_parents` receipts from active branch children, and `verify-event-parent-delivery.ts` proves those receipts surface through `notifications_master.php`. `notifications_master`-style grouped notification payloads now load uncapped alarm, receipt, event, message, and assessment detail arrays for the selected child/parent instead of sampling the first 100 rows per family. Remaining work is alarm-family push delivery, offline/mobile polish, native-device audit, and exact production `notifications_nature` acceptance after import. |
